--创建获取ID的函数
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION F_GET_SEQ_CORE
RETURN number
is
v_seq number:=0;
BEGIN
  SELECT t.id INTO v_seq FROM T_CORE_SEQ_CORE t;
  UPDATE T_CORE_SEQ_CORE t SET t.id = t.id + 1;
  RETURN v_seq+1;
END;

alter table T_CORE_ORG add tempcolumn CLOB;--添加临时字段
update T_CORE_ORG set tempcolumn="ORGNAME";--将原字段数据复制到临时字段中
update T_CORE_ORG set ORGNAME=NULL;--将原字段数据清空
alter table T_CORE_ORG drop column ORGNAME;--将原字段删除
alter table T_CORE_ORG add ORGNAME VARCHAR2(500) NULL; --重新加原字段
update T_CORE_ORG set ORGNAME = tempcolumn;--将临时字段数据复制到修改后的原字段
alter table T_CORE_ORG drop column tempcolumn;--删除临时字段
commit;

--创建获取ID的函数
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION F_GET_SEQ_CORE
RETURN number
is
v_seq number:=0;
BEGIN
  SELECT t.id INTO v_seq FROM T_CORE_SEQ_CORE t;
  UPDATE T_CORE_SEQ_CORE t SET t.id = t.id + 1;
  RETURN v_seq+1;
END;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE t_emc_samplenumber(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ORGID VARCHAR2(16)  NULL,
    REPORTCONFIGLINEID NUMBER NULL,
    NUMBERCODE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER IS '采样单编码信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.ORGID IS '所属单位ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.REPORTCONFIGLINEID IS '报表ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.NUMBERCODE IS '采样单编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER PRIMARY KEY (ID);


-- 创建菜单语句
INSERT INTO T_CORE_MENU (ID, MENUNAME, HREF, PARENTID, ORDERNO, ICON, MENUINITNAME)
VALUES ('100542', '采样单编码信息', '/secure/emc/module/mdm/bizmdm/samplenumbers/page/edit-list', '1005', '100542', '035.svg', '采样单编码信息');

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT)
VALUES ('T_EMC_SAMPLENUMBER', '0', '0');


COMMIT;

-- -----------------------------2021-06-17 add by zhengxy--------------begin

-- 测试方法默认QC
CREATE TABlE T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC (
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TESTMETHODID NUMBER NULL,
    QCCATEGORYID NUMBER NULL,
    QCCATEGORYCODE VARCHAR2(18) NULL,
    QCNAME VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    ADDNUM INT NULL,
    STARTORDERINDEX INT NULL,
    INTERVALNUM INT NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) NULL
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC IS '测试方法默认QC';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.id IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.TESTMETHODID IS '测试方法ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.QCCATEGORYID IS 'QC类型ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.QCCATEGORYCODE IS 'QC类型编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.QCNAME IS 'QC名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.ADDNUM IS '添加QC数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_DEFAULT_QC.STARTORDERINDEX IS '起始样品';

-- 关联测试方法
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD (
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TESTMETHODID NUMBER NULL,
    RELATEDTESTMETHODID NUMBER NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) NULL
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD IS '关联测试方法';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.TESTMETHODID IS '测试方法ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.RELATEDTESTMETHODID IS '关联测试方法ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD ADD CONSTRAINT T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD PRIMARY KEY (ID);

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE(ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_EMC_RELATED_TEST_METHOD', '0', '0');

COMMIT;

--新增VOC空瓶表
CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING"
(       "ID" VARCHAR2(32) NOT NULL ENABLE,
        "VOCNUMBER"   VARCHAR2(200),
        "WEIGHINGUSERNAME" VARCHAR2(200),
        "EMPTYBOTTLEWEIGHT"  VARCHAR2(200) ,
        "WEIGHTTIME"  DATE,
		"PROJNO" VARCHAR2(16),
		 "CREATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(32),
		 "CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64),
		 "CREATEDTIME" DATE,
		 "CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(32),
		 "CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING IS 'VOC瓶称重表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.VOCNUMBER IS 'VOC瓶号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.WEIGHINGUSERNAME IS '称重人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.EMPTYBOTTLEWEIGHT IS '空瓶重量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.WEIGHTTIME IS '称重时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING.PROJNO IS '项目编号';

INSERT INTO T_CORE_SEQUENCE(ID, PATTERN, JSONPARAM) VALUES ('T_EMC_VOC_WEIGHTING', '[key][nnnnnnn]', NULL);
COMMIT;

-- 同步数据
UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT A SET (CUSTOMERNAME, CUSTOMERCONTACT, CUSTOMERPHONE, INSPECTEDCONTACT, INSPECTEDPHONE, SAMPLINGLOCATION) = (SELECT CUSTOMERNAME, CUSTOMERCONTACT, CUSTOMERPHONE, INSPECTEDCONTCTS AS INSPECTEDCONTACT, INSPECTEDPHONE, DRAWAREA AS SAMPLINGLOCATION FROM T_CRM_OPPORTUNITY_MAIN B, T_CRM_OPPORTUNITY_CONTRACT C WHERE B.ID = C.OPPORTUNITYID AND A.CONTRACTNO = C.CONTRACTNO) WHERE CONTRACTNO IS NOT NULL;

COMMIT;

-- 分析仪器曲线表
CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES"
(
   ID NUMBER(20) NOT NULL,
  "RUNID"   VARCHAR2(200),
  "SDRESERVECONCENTRATION"   VARCHAR2(200),
  "SOLID" NUMBER(20),
  "SDUSEFLUIDCONCENTRATION"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "CURVEFACTORFIXEDTO"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "ADDDATE"  DATE,
  "ADDER"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "CREATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64),
  "CREATEDTIME" DATE,
  "CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES IS '分析仪器曲线表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.RUNID IS '批号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.SDRESERVECONCENTRATION IS '标准储备液浓度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.SOLID IS '标准溶液批号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.SDUSEFLUIDCONCENTRATION IS '标准使用液浓度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CURVEFACTORFIXEDTO IS '曲线系数定容至';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.ADDDATE IS '添加日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.ADDER IS '添加人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_CRVES.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL"
(
  ID NUMBER(20) NOT NULL,
  "RUNID"   VARCHAR2(200),
  "SOLID" NUMBER(20),
  "SDSUBSTANCES"   VARCHAR2(200),
  "ANALYSISNUMBER"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "SDSOLUTIONVOLUME"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "SDCONCENTRATION"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "REMARK"  VARCHAR2(200),
  "CREATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64),
  "CREATEDTIME" DATE,
  "CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256)
);
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL IS '分析仪器曲线明细表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.RUNID IS '批号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.SOLID IS '标准溶液批号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.SDSUBSTANCES IS '标准物质';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.ANALYSISNUMBER IS '分析编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.SDSOLUTIONVOLUME IS '标准溶液加入体积ml';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.SDCONCENTRATION IS '标准物质浓度ug/L';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.REMARK IS '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ANALYTICAL_EQUIPT_DETAIL.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

-- -----------------------------2021-12-08 add by zhengxy--------------begin

-- QC关联的样品
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER (
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    QCORDERID NUMBER,
    QCORDERNO VARCHAR2(36),
    QCCATEGORYID NUMBER,
    QCCATEGORYCODE VARCHAR2(36),
    QCNAME VARCHAR2(36),
    RELATEDORDERID NUMBER,
    RELATEDORDERNO VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60),
    CREATEDTIME DATE,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER IS 'QC关联的样品';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.QCORDERID IS 'QC样品ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.QCORDERNO IS 'QC样品编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.QCCATEGORYID IS 'QC ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.QCCATEGORYCODE IS 'QC编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.QCNAME IS 'QC名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.RELATEDORDERID IS '关联的样品ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.RELATEDORDERNO IS '关联的样品编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER ADD CONSTRAINT T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER PRIMARY KEY (ID);

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE(ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_EMC_QC_RELATED_ORDER', '0', '0');

COMMIT;

-- 报告明细表
CREATE TABlE T_EMC_REPORT_LINE (
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    REPORTID NUMBER,
    PROJID NUMBER,
    FOLDERID NUMBER,
    ORDERID NUMBER,
    ORDERTASKID NUMBER,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60),
    CREATEDTIME DATE,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_REPORT_LINE IS '报告明细表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.REPORTID IS '报告ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.PROJID IS '项目ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.FOLDERID IS '点位ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.ORDERID IS '样品ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.ORDERTASKID IS '样品测试ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_LINE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_OIL_FILM(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    OLIFILMNO VARCHAR2(40),
    WEIGHINGUSERID NUMBER(20),
    WEIGHINGUSERNAME VARCHAR2(40),
    PROJNO VARCHAR2(40),
    WEIGHINGDATE DATE,
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEONE VARCHAR2(40),
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONETWO VARCHAR2(40),
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONETHREE VARCHAR2(40),
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEFOUR VARCHAR2(40),
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEFIVE VARCHAR2(40),
    EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONESIX VARCHAR2(40),
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60),
    CREATEDTIME DATE,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270),
    ORGID VARCHAR2(20)
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_OIL_FILM IS '油膜滤筒信息表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.OLIFILMNO IS '滤筒或油膜编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.WEIGHINGUSERID IS '称重人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.WEIGHINGUSERNAME IS '称重人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.PROJNO IS '项目编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.WEIGHINGDATE IS '称重时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEONE IS '空滤桶M1-1(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONETWO IS '空滤桶M1-2(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONETHREE IS '空滤桶M1-3(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEFOUR IS '空滤桶M1-4(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONEFIVE IS '空滤桶M1-5(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.EMPTYFILTERBARRELMONESIX IS '空滤桶M1-6(g)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_OIL_FILM.ORGID IS '父单位编码';



COMMIT;

UPDATE T_CORE_CODE SET CODE = '<' WHERE CODECATEGORYID = 'highLimitSymbol' AND CODENAME = '<';
UPDATE T_CORE_CODE SET CODE = '<=' WHERE CODECATEGORYID = 'highLimitSymbol' AND CODENAME = '<=';

UPDATE T_CORE_CODE SET CODE = '/' WHERE CODECATEGORYID = 'lowLimitSymbol' AND CODENAME = '/';
UPDATE T_CORE_CODE SET CODE = '>' WHERE CODECATEGORYID = 'lowLimitSymbol' AND CODENAME = '>';
UPDATE T_CORE_CODE SET CODE = '>=' WHERE CODECATEGORYID = 'lowLimitSymbol' AND CODENAME = '>=';

-- 更新分析项分段修约上下限符号
UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '/' WHERE LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '1';
UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '>' WHERE LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '2';
UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '>=' WHERE LOWLIMITSYMBOL = '3';

UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '/' WHERE HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '1';
UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '<' WHERE HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '2';
UPDATE T_EMC_ITEM_REVISION SET HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '<=' WHERE HIGHLIMITSYMBOL = '3';

 );

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    SAMPLEBOTTLENO VARCHAR2(200)  NULL,
    WEIGHINGUSERNAME VARCHAR2(200)  NULL,
    EMPTYBOTTLEWEIGHT VARCHAR2(200)  NULL,
    WEIGHTTIME DATE NULL,
    PROJNO VARCHAR2(200)  NULL,
    ORGID VARCHAR2(200) NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE IS '采样瓶表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.SAMPLEBOTTLENO IS '采样瓶编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.WEIGHINGUSERNAME IS '称重人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.EMPTYBOTTLEWEIGHT IS '空瓶重量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.WEIGHTTIME IS '称重时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.PROJNO IS '项目编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE PRIMARY KEY (ID);

INSERT INTO T_CORE_SEQUENCE(ID, PATTERN, JSONPARAM) VALUES ('T_EMC_SAMPLE_BOTTLE', '[key][nnnnnnn]', NULL);

COMMIT;
